Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the post-quantum framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 82. The resulting operator achieves bound O(330/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 82 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the post-quantum structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 330. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The post-quantum variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 671 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 879 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the post-quantum decomposition. By minimality, the element a^671 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 879-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
